{"antonyms":[{"word":"every"},{"word":"the"}],"etymology_number":"2","lang_code":"en","pos":"article","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"There was a man here looking for you yesterday.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An unspecified example of (something); the indefinite article."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I've seen it happen a hundred times.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One; used before score, dozen, hundred, thousand, million, etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's a bit thick, isn't he?","type":"example"},{"text":"They asked me a few questions.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used in some phrases denoting quantity, such as a few, a good many, a couple, a little, a bit, etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"There are a few hundred orders that need to be fulfilled by tomorrow.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used in some phrases denoting quantity, such as a few, a good many, a couple, a little, a bit, etc.","Used before a numeral."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The door was opened a little.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used in some adverbial phrases denoting the degree or extent of an action, such as a little, a bit, a lot, etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"We are of a mind on matters of morals.","type":"example"},{"text":"They're two of a kind.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The same; one and the same. Used in phrases such as of a kind, birds of a feather, etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"A man who dies intestate leaves his children troubles and difficulties.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any; every; used before a noun which has become modified to limit its scope."]},{"examples":[{"text":"It was so dark that we couldn't see a thing.","type":"example"},{"text":"He fell all that way, and hasn't a bump on his head?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any; used with a negative to indicate not a single one."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The lottery jackpot is worth a staggering three hundred million dollars.","type":"example"},{"text":"The holidays are a mere one week away.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used before an adjective that modifies a noun (singular or plural) delimited by a numeral."]},{"examples":[{"text":"We've received an interesting letter from a Mrs. Miggins of London.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One; someone named; used before a person's name, suggesting that the speaker knows little about the person other than the name."]},{"glosses":["Used before an adjective modifying a person's name, typically used to emphasize that person's current condition or emotional state."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The center of the village was becoming a Times Square.","type":"example"},{"text":"The man is a regular Romeo.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Someone or something like; similar to; used before a proper noun to create an example out of it."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/eɪ/"},{"ipa":"/ə/","tags":["unstressed"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"an"},{"word":"one"}],"upstream_line":322113,"upstream_offset":1597122932,"upstream_record_sha256":"dfbdf9d9dc9ec8610db264089dbf6072b49b1454d19c33b545cf96003556d794","word":"a"}

{"forms":[{"form":"riveters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A person whose job is to rivet."],"topics":["arts","business","construction","crafts","engineering","hobbies","lifestyle","manufacturing","metallurgy","metalworking","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Rosie the Riveter is an iconic piece of American WWII propaganda.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person whose job is to rivet.","A gunner; the member of a riveting team who uses a rivet gun, fitted with a rivet set which matches the shape of the factory head of a solid rivet, to impact that rivet (which is heated if necessary to soften it), while the bucker (or holder-up) holds a heavy bucking bar against the bucktail of the rivet, upsetting it to form the field head. If the rivets have to be heated, the team will also include a cook and a catcher; see rivet for more details."],"raw_tags":["for solid rivets"],"tags":["specifically"],"topics":["arts","business","construction","crafts","engineering","hobbies","lifestyle","manufacturing","metallurgy","metalworking","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"de-riveter"}],"examples":[{"text":"The riveter required a compressed air supply.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A tool used to fix rivets."],"synonyms":[{"word":"rivet gun"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹɪvətə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹɪvətɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":1092282,"upstream_offset":3879990238,"upstream_record_sha256":"a34b5e0169c8f601dfb313ab1e850faaa9ae12f89c1612984b03829d9b711201","word":"riveter"}
